##FILE-BEGIN## trading-research/pipeline/README.md (1989 bytes)
# AVFI-NQ quant research repo

Strategy-phase repository for the AVFI-NQ weekly programme. Governing
document: the trading-research knowledge topic's programme file (v1.0 plus
Amendment 1). The working tree is ephemeral across environment resets; the
durable snapshot lives in the trading-research knowledge topic under
pipeline/. Read research/PROGRESS.md first.

## Layout

- strategies/avfi_v3_reference.pine: Andrea's indicator, verbatim.
- strategies/avfi_replication.py: pandas port, Pine-exact conventions.
  compute_avfi(df, **overrides), all script parameters overridable.
- strategies/backtest.py: weekly-bar engine. Close decisions, next-open
  fills, close-fill sensitivity, cost_per_side parameter (stage 5 stress),
  2x ATR(20) initial stops, intraweek stop checks.
- research/stage0c_canonical.py: Yahoo daily -> canonical weekly bars,
  repair log, thin-volume flags.
- research/stage1_replication.py: 7 unit tests + indicator series export.
- research/stage2_eventstudy.py: H1/H2 event study, block bootstrap.
- research/stage3_backtest.py: rule set A variants vs controls C1/C2/C3.
- research/stage4a_sensitivity.py: H4, H5 grid, H6, normWin=150 variant.
- research/stage5_coststress.py: slippage stress 1-3 ticks.
- research/stage6a_registry.py: trial registry, PSR, individual bootstrap
  p-values, White reality check over the grid family.
- research/PROGRESS.md: append-only stage log with all verdicts.
- research/*_report.json: machine-readable stage outputs.
- data/: nq_weekly_canonical.csv, avfi_series.csv.

## Rules

- Hypotheses H1-H6 and rule sets A/B/C frozen per the programme document;
  H7 and rule set T registered per Amendment 1.
- Holdout 2021+ sealed; opened exactly once, for the registered battery,
  on Andrea's explicit go. Nothing else may touch it.
- No parameter re-optimisation on registered rules, ever.
- Every variant run is logged in PROGRESS.md with its verdict.
- Paper trading only. Simulated profitability is never proof of an edge.

##FILE-END## trading-research/pipeline/README.md
